## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a test that keeps failing in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 사용자님의 개인 AI 파트너로서 이 컴퓨터에서 코드 작업, 웹 확인, 메일·캘린더 확인 같은 일을 맡을 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 바로 시작할게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 굉장히 간단한 인사라 도구 없이 바로 답할게요.
  [reminder set] 내일 아침에 다시 이야기하기로 한 것 기억하라고 알려드려요
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 오전 9시에 알림 드릴게요. 오늘 푹 쉬세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봬요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは速さを優先する通信です。

TCPは届いたか・順番どおりか を確認しながら送るので、確実に届きますが、その分遅くなります。UDPは確認を省いて送るので、速いものの届かないことや順番が入れ替わることがあります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de integrarla, siempre que nadie más la haya usado. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque reescribir commits que otros tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的、面向连接的传输，会确认和重传数据；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for your own branch that hasn't been shared yet. Merge when the branch is shared or you want to keep the true history of how the work came together.
```

  [partner ack line] 好，我去携程查国贸附近的酒店和周一到周三上海飞北京的机票，查清楚了告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 携程：下周一至周三北京出差的酒店、机票和总价 | place: {"kind":"browser","profile":"default","device":"local"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (sonnet) | brief: 用户原话："下周一到周三去北京出差，帮我在携程看看国贸附近的酒店，再看看那几天上海飞北京的机票，最后算一下一共多少钱" ⏎  ⏎ 已定的假设（今天是 2026-11-20 周五）： ⏎ - 下周一 2026-11-23，周二 11-24，周三 11-25。 ⏎ - 酒店：入住 2026-11-23，退房 2026-11-26，共 3 晚，地点选北京国贸（CBD）附近。 ⏎ - 机票：上海（虹桥或浦东均可）飞北京（首都或大兴均可）的单程，分别查 11-23、11-24、11-25 三天，每天列出最便宜的 2–3 个航班（航司、起飞时间、价格）。 ⏎  ⏎ 要做的： ⏎ 1. 在携程查国贸附近的酒店，按价格从低到高列出 3–5 家（酒店名、星级或评分、每晚价格、3 晚总价、距国贸大概距离）。 ⏎ 2. 查上面三天的上海到北京机票，列出每天最便宜的 2–3 个航班。 ⏎ 3. 算总价：3 晚最便宜的酒店价 + 周一那趟最便宜的机票价，并写清算法。另外给出如果三天都要飞，总价是多少。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准：报告里列出酒店清单、三天机票清单、两个总价数字，并注明价格查询时间。 ⏎  ⏎ 不做的事：不下单、不预订、不付款、不登录账号；只看公开页面。价格有变化或需要登录才能看的，直接写明，不要替用户做选择。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：用户电脑上的 Ducter 自己的浏览器，打开携程网站即可，不需要用户的个人账号。

--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径放在后面就行：

```sh
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

如果只想跑某一个用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```sh
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database writes down each change before applying it to the main data. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays the log on restart to recover the changes that were committed and roll back the ones that weren't.
```

  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁周五票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1831 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海，600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" style="display:none">还没有票价记录</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var count = document.getElementById('count');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];
    });
  }
  function render(trains) {
    trains = Array.isArray(trains) ? trains : [];
    if (!trains.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '';
      empty.style.display = '';
      count.textContent = '0 班';
      sub.textContent = '还没查到';
      ducter.summary('还没查到');
      return;
    }
    empty.style.display = 'none';
    var cheap = trains.filter(function (t) { return Number(t.price) < 600; });
    count.textContent = cheap.length + ' 班';
    sub.textContent = '共查到 ' + trains.length + ' 班';
    ducter.summary(cheap.length + ' 班 600 元以下');
    list.innerHTML = trains.map(function (t) {
      var ok = Number(t.price) < 600;
      return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' +
        '<div>' + esc(t.no) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(t.seat || '二等座') + (t.left != null ? ' · 余 ' + esc(t.left) + ' 张' : '') + '</div>' +
        '</div><div class="k-row-value" style="color:' + (ok ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)') + '">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(render);
  }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"left":12,"seat":"二等座"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"left":4,"seat":"二等座"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"left":null,"seat":"二等座"}]}

  [partner message] Done. The board is ready and filled with nights 2 and 3. Each new night's result will add a row, and the latest number sits at the top.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1835 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest run</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">Not run yet</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latestSub">Each night's write rate shows here</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
function el(tag, cls, text) {
  var e = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) e.className = cls;
  if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
  return e;
}
function render() {
  var runs = (ducter.get('runs') || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.night - a.night; });
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
  var sub = document.getElementById('latestSub');
  list.textContent = '';
  if (!runs.length) {
    latest.className = 'k-sum-value k-none';
    latest.textContent = 'Not run yet';
    sub.textContent = "Each night's write rate shows here";
    list.appendChild(el('div', 'k-empty', 'Each night will add a row here.'));
    ducter.summary('No runs yet');
    return;
  }
  var r = runs[0];
  latest.className = 'k-sum-value';
  latest.textContent = r.writesPerSec + ' writes/s';
  sub.textContent = 'Night ' + r.night + ' · p99 ' + r.p99ms + ' ms · ' + r.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
  runs.forEach(function (x) {
    var row = el('div', 'k-row');
    var main = el('div', 'k-row-main');
    main.appendChild(el('div', '', 'Night ' + x.night));
    main.appendChild(el('div', 'k-row-sub', 'p99 ' + x.p99ms + ' ms · ' + x.stalls + ' compaction stalls'));
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(el('div', 'k-row-value', x.writesPerSec + '/s'));
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(runs.length + ' nights · latest ' + r.writesPerSec + ' writes/s');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":2,"writesPerSec":"181k","p99ms":"4.0","stalls":0},{"night":3,"writesPerSec":"182k","p99ms":"4.1","stalls":0}]}

  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>

  <details class="k-add" id="add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>时间</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="如 14:30，留空为现在">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>奶量 (ml)</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="如 120">
    </div>
    <div class="k-chips">
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="60">60</button>
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="90">90</button>
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="120">120</button>
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="150">150</button>
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
    <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删掉这一顿</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  let editId = null;

  function today() {
    const d = new Date();
    return d.get … return;
    const f = (ducter.get('feeds') || []).find(x => String(x.id) === r.dataset.id);
    if (!f) return;
    editId = f.id;
    $('t').value = f.t;
    $('ml').value = f.ml;
    $('del').hidden = false;
    $('save').textContent = '改好了';
    $('add').open = true;
  };

  document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = () => { $('ml').value = b.dataset.ml; };
  });

  $('save').onclick = () => {
    const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
    if (!ml || ml <= 0) return;
    let t = $('t').value.trim();
    if (!t) {
      const d = new Date();
      t = pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes());
    }
    const m = t.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
    if (!m) return;
    t = pad(+m[1]) + ':' + m[2];

    const all = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
    if (editId != null) {
      const f = all.find(x => x.id === editId);
      if (f) { f.t = t; f.ml = ml; }
    } else {
      all.push({ id: Date.now(), d: today(), t: t, ml: ml });
    }
    ducter.set('feeds', all);
    reset();
  };

  $('del').onclick = () => {
    ducter.set('feeds', (ducter.get('feeds') || []).filter(x => x.id !== editId));
    reset();
  };

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"d":"2026-11-21","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"d":"2026-11-21","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

function init(v){
  prs = Array.isArray(v) && v.length ? v : seed.map(function(p){return Object.assign({}, p);});
  render();
}
document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function(e){
  var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if(!row) return;
  prs.forEach(function(p){ if(p.id === row.getAttribute('data-id')) p.done = !p.done; });
  ducter.set('prs', prs);
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(function(){
  var v = ducter.get('prs');
  if (v && v.then) v.then(function(x){ if(Array.isArray(x)){ prs = x; render(); } });
  else if (Array.isArray(v)) { prs = v; render(); }
});
var got = ducter.get('prs');
if (got && got.then) got.then(init); else init(got);

### direct-stable-fact-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: Bun 是用什么语言写的？
  [partner message] Bun 主要用 **Zig** 写，运行时核心基本都是 Zig。它的 JavaScript 引擎用的是 WebKit 的 **JavaScriptCore**，这部分是 C++，Bun 通过绑定来调用。另外还有一些 C/C++ 代码，部分内置模块则是用 TypeScript 写的。
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 卡通笔记本电脑，黑蓝色的边框，屏幕是空白的，键盘和触控板都画得很清楚。风格偏柔和，像软陶质感。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好表示“这里还没有内容”，而且看起来亲切、不吓人。用的时候注意两点：一是最好配一句说明，比如“还没有记录，点这里新建一个”，不然空屏幕只会让人觉得页面坏了；二是它的配色偏暖，如果你的界面是冷色调或很鲜艳的，放进去可能有点不搭。显示尺寸控制在 120 到 200 像素宽比较合适，图只有 320 像素，放大会发糊。
```
